(position, content) 
  (1, 'The antiwhite suicide squad is dead silent because it doesn''t help or harm the wellbeing of White people.'),
  (2, 'Who are the villains in her story?
Straight White males.

Climatism, LGBT and feminism are all different variations of antiwhitism.

Burn her down by naming the witch antiwhite.'),
  (3, 'Antiwhites have pretexts instead of principles.

If the conclusion is that Whites must be victimized for their birth the pretexts don''t matter, it is antiwhite and must be rejected.'),
  (4, 'Inclusive" is just an antiwhite pretext for white erasure."'),
  (5, 'We don''t need the approval of antiwhites to advocate for the wellbeing of our people.'),
  (6, 'That want didn''t come naturally. 

It is a consequence of the antiwhite narrative that tells them they should find safety in their sex instead of in their people. With no man to protect them they turn to the state.'),
  (7, 'Multiracialism is white erasure.'),
  (8, 'White erasure is antiwhite, so no.'),
  (9, 'Antiwhite pig.'),
  (10, 'The only victimization by society is antiwhitism.'),
  (11, 'White erasure harms White wellbeing.

That''s all that''s needed to defend against antiwhitism.'),
  (12, 'I''ve observed this behavior many times.

Pristine environments is a Western norm not shared with all.
White erasure creates a cultural arms race.'),
  (13, 'An antiwhite pig willing to endure the gang rapes white erasure will bring as long as White males are put into the ground.'),
  (14, 'He takes the name of their giving (far-right") and gives them the name of their taking ("anti-racist") framing the whole conversation inside the antiwhite narrative.'),
  (17, 'There is no Western civilization without Westernkind.

It has to be recreated every minute by White people projecting and enforcing our norms of civility.

When you white erase our people you destroy our civilization.'),
  (18, 'Yes, the defense against antiwhites framing our victimization as wellbeing for others is easy to get wrong. Always focus on White wellbeing.

For example, instead of putting the focus on the diversity hire, talk about the White male who was denied his future life for his birth.'),
  (19, 'You''re not talking to the antiwhite, your talking to the audience.

Whites don''t know how to defend themselves from a moral position. When the learn that the most White guilted woman will defend her people with righteous fury. I witnessed that with my own wife.'),
  (20, 'Being called antiwhite slurs is an offensive opportunity to get the attacker to confirm/deny his or her antiwhitism.

Smart antiwhites will just disengage. Once they deny it you define what they must do to prove it.'),
  (21, 'Antiwhites frame our victimization as wellbeing for others or retributive justice.

Both are defeated by the same super weapon:

If the conclusion is that Whites must be victimized for their birth the pretexts don''t matter, it is antiwhite and must be rejected.'),
  (22, 'Whites require a moral framework to function. We cannot change that. 

What we can change is to replace antiwhitism with a White positive social morality where the wellbeing of White people takes priority.'),
  (23, 'In the antiwhite narrative Whites did slavery.

In reality everyone did until White men ended it.'),
  (24, 'Yes, the battle we have to win is inside the minds of White people. Once we win them over we win everything.
The Go Free practice is the blueprint for the recapture of our destiny:'),
  (25, 'It''s not worse. Worse is a friend of mine waking up when the knife entered his chest.

He was out of the fight in seconds and only lived to tell the tale because his wife emptied his .357 Magnum that was under the cushion into the antiwhite monster.'),
  (26, 'In SA we have an antiwhite regime that dials that up to eleven yet Whites are more White guilted than anywhere else.
All it does is make it more difficult for people like me to keep functioning and get the message out to our people.
More antiwhitism is never better.'),
  (27, 'Accelerationists think if we choose a government that has a genocidal hatred for Whites with members of parlaiment calling for the murdering of White women and children that Whites will wake up.

They don''t because Whites are not a people, we are a group of radical individuals.'),
  (28, 'When you create your next account try to spell scrotum correctly.'),
  (29, 'We''ll deport all antiwhites of all races. Give them the group discount.'),
  (30, 'White erasure harms White wellbeing.

That''s the moral position that will stand the test of time.'),
  (31, 'No woman may choose to end a pregnancy unless it was rape, the baby is defective or her life is at risk.

To suggest that this position is motivated by anything other than the wellbeing of the mother or the body of our people is despicable.

Stop talking.'),
  (32, 'White antiwhites will have to be deported with the rest of the infinity intruders.

They voted themselves out of the conversation when they decided it''s OK to target White babies for victimization while still in the womb.'),
  (33, 'We need to win back the West where it was defeated, at the dinner table, the coffee machine and the Christmas party.

If the conclusion is that Whites must be victimized for their birth the pretexts don''t matter, it is antiwhite and must be rejected.'),
  (34, 'Antiwhites could all do with a trip to a day spa in Haiti to clear their thoughts.'),
  (35, 'We don''t have privilege. We earned it and we have the right to inherit and to pass it down.'),
  (36, 'How about a complimentary deportation for any antiwhite who interferes?'),
  (37, 'The argument for abortion of fertilized eggs does not apply to consensual sex.

It''s for rape and Downs babies, when the mother is at risk and to allow the IVF process.

After the first trimester the baby can survive without the mother.'),
  (38, 'Before the white erasure of our norms in the sixties first trimester abortions were only allowed for rape and Downs pregnancies and if the mother''s life was at risk.

Elective abortion" is infanticide. The decision to have unprotected sex takes on responsibility for a new life."'),
  (39, 'White erasure.'),
  (40, 'Every straw man is a victory for me.'),
  (41, 'Forcing diversity hires to reapply for their positions should be the default since they were not hired for excellence.'),
  (42, 'White erasing Western civilization with infinity intruders is not irrelevant.

  (43, 'It''s antiwhite for white erase.'),
  (44, 'Antiwhites don''t care about skin tone. They have no problem with the Chinese.

  (45, 'The only victimization by society is antiwhitism.'),
  (46, 'An antiwhite pig insulting our cultural inheritance.'),
  (47, 'The white erasure of our Western norms of civility is his job, his right to exist. 

Antiwhites invite infinity intruders in to harm the physical, financial, psychological and spiritual wellbeing of White people.'),
  (48, 'We don''t know when but we have boundary conditions. 

Abortion after the first trimester is infanticide. That''s where the fight is between the antiwhite left and the antiwhite lite right.

The house is on fire. We can choose the color of the curtains if we have a house left.'),
  (49, 'South Africa is a lesson to accelerationists that worse isn''t better, worse is just worse.

Whites don''t wake up when the world burns because the remedy suggested by both sides is always more antiwhitism. Like you are doing right now by preferring Kamala.'),
  (50, 'After the first trimester the womb''s work is mostly done as evidenced by the survival of healthy premature babies.'),
  (51, 'There are no easy answers. When does an Alzheimer''s patient stop being a person? At some point the body becomes an empty shell with bodily functions.
A heart rate doesn''t mean consciousness.

Growing a baby is the same process in reverse.'),
  (52, 'Antiwhite pigs think nothing ever happens to them.'),
  (53, 'Antiwhite policies that white erase society benefits all nonwhites, even the Chinese.'),
  (54, 'Antiwhites don''t deserve access to White people.

  (55, 'White is whoever can create Western civilization.

If you don''t recognize Western civilization in the Roman empire you''re not as White as you think.'),
  (56, 'It happens somewhere between conception and the end of the first trimester.
We know that because we have babies who survived exiting the womb as early as 26 weeks. Technology, i.e. the White man''s magic, will push that back but there will be a point where you hit a wall.'),
  (57, 'You have a simplistic understanding of human life.

  (58, 'Exorcising starts by naming the demon antiwhitism.'),
  (59, 'If we let the antiwhite welfare state run amok all the adults we produce will eventually talk like this.

  (60, 'That''s the norm currently but I think a better one would be to not allow it at any time unless the sex was not consensual or the baby is defective.'),
  (61, 'Antiwhites are psychologically unable to say our name except when they target us for victimization.

  (62, 'If you cannot say Jesus loves White people you are antiwhite.

  (64, 'Newsom is antiwhite for Nero.'),

  (66, 'After the first trimester it''s infanticide unless the pregnancy is because of rape.

  (68, 'The clustering of positions around Western norms or the rejection of them is because the true divide is between Whites who naturally resist the white erasure of our expressions on the one side and antiwhites on the other.'),

They will lie, cheat and break their own laws because the are true believers.'),
  (70, 'Inoculate your child against antiwhitism or they will opt out of the White nuclear family in a pathetic attempt to atone for grievances never committed.'),

  (74, 'The anti-nonwhite movements are all antiwhite cancer without exception.

  (77, 'Objecting to molesting is a Western norm not shared with all.

  (85, 'White erasure by White female meets white erasure by infinity intruder.'),

  (94, 'We need the best of the diamond hard meat eating men of the West to face the evil of our age.

  (110, 'The brown boy groypers are antiwhite operatives whose right to exist is to link the wellbeing of White people to the detriment of others.

  (111, 'I think it''s probably safe to say she was never hit in the face during her training. She''s probably very good at verbal de-escalation, it''s just unfortunate that she couldn''t get a word in.'),
  (112, 'Westernkind puts the finger on what makes us White.

  (116, 'The importance of language. If the Grounded Commonwealth mod made its vision to remove white erasure and antiwhitism, antiwhites would have been in the immoral position. This review censored the mod name so that no one can find it.'),

  (118, 'Die ekonomie is die lat waarmee antiwittes geslaan word.

  (121, 'African Growth" is antiwhite for white erasure."'),
  (122, 'Where in the White world does it become dangerous for nonwhites when the sun goes down?

  (132, 'White men created a nursery room and called it Western civilization.

  (136, 'White women are not told they have a people to be proud of. Not told they are the only beings able to give birth to the creator of Western civilization.

  (137, 'They interpret everything through the lens of their antiwhite morality.'),
  (138, 'America is Western civilization.

  (139, 'If the antiwhite regime was at all concerned about the wellbeing of their own people they would end the white erasure that is at the root of infrastructure collapse.

  (140, 'The bill for multiracialism always goes to White people.

  (141, 'Also known as heretics to antiwhitism.'),
  (142, 'We have the moral super weapon to defeat arguments that frame our victimization as retributive justice. We just need practice in wielding it:

  (146, 'Die antiwit regime sien wit verwydering en die vernietiging van die Westerse beskawing as sy morele opdrag.

  (180, 'White people were convinced that our norms, history and continuation are evil and they are taking steps to effectuate antiwhite outcomes.

  (464, 'antiwhitism is not a French problem. It''s the social morality of the West.'),
  (465, 'America is Western civilization. It did not exist before White people created it.

Nonwhites are guests who change their status to unwelcome when they turn antiwhite.'),
  (466, 'The history of Western civilization is rich with examples of antiwhite monsters making the calamitous mistake to think that we will submit like they do when we are faced with overwhelming numbers.'),
  (467, 'I''ll never forget standing by when my father put down my sisters little dog after he was mauled by a Rottweiler.

  (468, 'The problem is much deeper.

  (469, 'Wit verwydering en antiwitisme sal toeneem tot ons die demoon op sy naam noem.'),
  (470, 'Antiwhites tell our children they were born guilty into an evil race and must atone for what they did not do by opting out of the White nuclear family.

  (471, 'White youth are not told they have a people to be proud of. Not told that everything they see was invented by White men.

  (476, 'Antiwhites never call us White, at least not the smart ones. When they do, like Humza did, they get their clocks cleaned properly.

  (485, 'EMJ doesn''t care about White people.

  (486, 'You cannot outsource the competence lost by white erasure of public utilities because antiwhite policies force private institutions to implement the same white erasure that destroyed the culture of excellence everywhere.'),

  (488, 'Antiwhite slurs are used by the antiwhite regime to get us to self identify as the villain in the antiwhite narrative.
